Shared numbers that two PRs must never both take. Test tools, logs and results files refer to them, so a number is never reused or renumbered, even after its fix is removed.

## Reserving

`node tools/registry.ts reserve fixes <n-slots> --by <who> --what <text>` (or `reserve native <n-int32> ...`) takes a lock file next to the main checkout's `docs/registry.md`, reads the highest slot / version / offset from this worktree's and the main checkout's `docs/registry.md` **and** from `docs/data/registry-reservations.json` in the main checkout (found through `git rev-parse --git-common-dir`, so parallel worktrees that have not merged yet see each other), prints the range, records it in that file and adds a `reserved (<who>, <id>)` row plus a recomputed next-free row here. `list` shows the reservations and any number taken twice, `release <id>` drops one (a released top range is free again), `fix-next <file>` recomputes the next-free rows (`tools/agent-merge.cjs` runs it after merging this file), `--self-check` runs 12 concurrent reserves against a fixture.

## Rule

1. Reserve before you code with `tools/registry.ts` (above), and name the range in your issue comment.
2. Keep the `reserved` row in your PR; change it to the final meaning before merge.
3. Two PRs that reserved the same number: the first merged keeps it, the other rebases onto the next free one.
4. Gaps stay gaps (slots 19-20): old logs and results already read them as zero.

## Contributor ranges

The public repo lags the private one, so contributor PRs (currently alexvixell35-stack / Dalexlol) take new numbers only from their own ranges and never clash with ours. Existing numbers stay where they are.

| Kind | Contributor range | Maintainers use |
|---|---|---|
| `docs/NNN-*.md` | 500-599, then 600-899 (added 2026-11-17) | below 500 |
| docs/51 inference ledger `## N.` | §900-999, then §1000-1499 (added 2026-11-17) | below 900 |
| ts3fixes counter slots | 2000-2199 (full), 2200-2799 (2400-2799 added 2026-11-14), 2800-3999 (added 2026-11-17, full), 4000-4089 (added 2026-11-19; `kCounterSlots` = 4200; 4090-4199 hold fork-local test counters) | below 2000 |
| ts3native counter version blocks | v900+ | below v900 |

`node tools/registry.ts reserve --contributor <doc|ledger|fixes|native> ...` hands out the next free number of the range (same arguments as the normal `reserve`; without `--contributor` the ranges are skipped). Blocks are used in order, and a slot run never straddles a block end (2199/2200, 2799/2800): one that would not fit starts at the next block. ts3native version strings (`TS3NATIVE_VERSION`, changelog headings) are assigned by the maintainers at merge: a contributor PR writes "next version" and does not change the define. `tools/pr-preflight.ts` flags numbers outside the ranges and a changed version string (`--maintainer` turns that check off).

## Checking a branch before the PR

`node tools/pr-preflight.ts` (or `--pr <n>` for an open PR; `--base` defaults to `origin/main`) compares the branch with the base from their merge-base and lists only the problems:

- **doc numbers:** a new `docs/NNN-*.md` whose number the base already has, or that the branch uses twice, with the next free number (past the base's highest, skipping numbers other open PRs add; `gh` needed for those, `--no-prs` skips them). An open PR that adds the same new number is listed too: whichever merges second renumbers.
- **index lines:** lines the branch adds to `CLAUDE.md`, `PLAN.md` or `docs/index.md` that name a clashing doc number, with the number to use.
- **docs/51 ledger:** a new `## N.` section whose number the base gave another title or reserved in `docs/data/registry-reservations.json`, with the next free §.
- **counters:** ts3fixes slot rows and ts3native counters versions the branch adds that the base already uses (rows or reservations); reserve again with `tools/registry.ts`.
- **ts3native version:** a `TS3NATIVE_VERSION` bump the base already passed (with the next version), and version strings in added lines that the base has used since the branch started.
- **files:** files that would not merge (`git merge-tree`, git 2.38+); `--all` also lists files both sides changed that merge cleanly.
- **checks:** `tools/check-docs-code.ts` and the self-checks of the scripts the branch changed (`selftest-all.ts --only`); with `--pr` they run in a temporary worktree of the PR head. `--no-checks` skips them.

Read-only: it never commits, and uncommitted changes are not part of the check. `--all` prints what is fine as well, `--json` everything; the last line is `needs review: ...`, and the exit code is 1 when there is a problem.

## ts3native counters (`ts3native_counters` export, `Ts3nCounters` in `native/ts3native/ts3native.cpp`)

Append-only struct of `LONG` (int32). `version` (offset 0) tells readers how many fields exist; `tools/probe/agent.js` `nativeCounters` adds one field list per version. Offsets are bytes from the export.

| Version | ts3native | Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|---|---|
| v1 | 0.2.0 | 0x00-0x1C | `version`, `enabled`, `calls`, `hits`, `misses`, `stored`, `otherFails`, `windowMs` (route negative cache) |
| v2 | 0.3.0 | 0x20-0x3C | `workerMode` (switch), `workerReady`, `workerCalls`, `workerUs`, `workerMaxUs`, `waitUs`, `fallbacks`, `watchdogs` (route worker) |
| v3 | 0.4.0 | 0x40-0x60 | `budgetMode` (switch), `budgetReady`, `budgetCalls`, `budgetCapped`, `budgetCapNodes`, `budgetCapUs`, `budgetMaxNodes`, `budgetMaxNodesOk`, `budgetMaxUsOk` (route search budget) |
| v4 | 0.4.x (worker crash fix, docs/18) | 0x64-0x68 | `invokeHook`, `invokes` (managed invokes from the worker run on the caller thread) |
| v5 | 0.5.0 | 0x6C-0x90 | `asyncMode` (switch), `asyncReady`, `asyncStarted`, `asyncRefused`, `asyncCollected`, `asyncDropped`, `asyncUs`, `asyncMaxUs`, `asyncPumpUs`, `asyncPumpWaits` (async route plan) |
| v6 | 0.6.0 | 0x94-0xBC | `saveReady`, `saveMode` (switch), `saveJobs`, `savePromoted`, `saveFallbacks`, `saveCount`, `saveLastUs`, `saveLastWriteUs`, `saveLastBackupUs`, `saveLastCopyUs`, `saveLastFinalUs` (save write) |
| v7 | 0.7.0 | 0xC0-0xEC | `decompressReady`, `decompressMode` (switch), `decompressVerify` (switch), `decompressCalls`, `decompressFails`, `decompressMismatches`, `decompressMaxUs`, `decompressInMB`, `decompressOutMB`, `decompressMs`, `decompressMainCalls`, `decompressMainMs` (RefPack decoder) |
| v8 | 0.7.1 | 0xF0-0x100 | `hostCalls`, `planOffCalls`, `planOffFails`, `planOnCalls`, `planOnFails` (host calls proxied; plans per mode for A/B) |
| v9 | 0.8.0 | 0x104-0x12C | `allocReady`, `allocMode` (switch, +0x108), `allocHitsK`, `allocMissesK`, `allocCachedK`, `allocPassedK`, `allocThreads`, `allocFlushed`, `allocDropped`, `allocShutdowns`, `allocOomRetries` (allocator cache; `K` = units of 1024) |
| v10 | 0.9.0 | 0x130-0x148 | `waterJpsReady`, `waterJpsMode` (switch, +0x134), `waterJpsVerify` (switch), `waterJpsCallsK`, `waterJpsCellsK`, `waterJpsMismatches`, `waterJpsFallbacks` (water JPS) |
| v11 | 0.10.0 | 0x14C-0x198 | `compressReady`, `compressMode` (switch, +0x150), `compressVerify` (switch, +0x154), `compressBatches`, `compressJobs`, `compressHits`, `compressMisses`, `compressMismatches`, `compressInMB`, `compressWaitMs`, `compressLastMs`, `compressPeakMB` (save package compression on workers), `flushReady`, `flushMode` (switch, +0x180), `flushDeferred`, `flushJobs`, `flushWaits`, `flushWaitMs`, `flushLastMs`, `flushFails` (save flushes on a worker) |
| v12 | 0.10.1 | 0x19C-0x1AC | `compressCommits`, `compressCommitPending`, `compressDirectCalls`, `compressDirectMs`, `compressDirectMB` (where save compression runs: commit hook calls, codec compress calls outside a batch) |
| v13 | 0.10.2 | 0x1B0-0x1C8 | `heightReady`, `heightMode` (switch, +0x1B4), `heightCalls`, `heightHits`, `heightMisses`, `heightRebuilds`, `heightMismatches` (terrain height cache) |
| v14 | 0.10.2 | 0x1CC-0x1D8 | `gcCheckReady`, `gcOwnerTid`, `gcForeignAllocs`, `gcForeignTid` (GC allocation thread check, measurement) |
| v15 | 0.10.2 | 0x1DC-0x200 | `icLockReady`, `icLockEntersK`, `icLockContended`, `icLockWaitUs`, `icLockMaxWaitUs`, `icLockOwnerChanges`, `icLockPrevTid`, `icLockLastTid`, `icLockLeaveWaiters`, `icLockCallback` (Mono loader lock check, measurement) |
| v16 | 0.10.3 | 0x204-0x278 | `rgReady`, `rgLock`, `rgOwnerTid`, `rgAcquires`, `rgEntries`, `rgUntracked`, `rgUnlocked[12]` (+0x21C), `rgJob[12]` (+0x24C); arrays in docs/18 S0 gate-site order, agent.js names them `rgUnlocked_<site>` / `rgJob_<site>` after `RL_GATES` (route gate measurement, docs/18 S2a) |
| v17 | 0.10.3 | 0x27C-0x2A0 | `cacheReady`, `cacheMode` (switch), `cacheHits`, `cacheMisses`, `cacheMismatches`, `cacheEvicted`, `cacheEntries`, `cacheKB`, `cacheHitMB`, `cacheHashMs` (save compressed-bytes cache, docs/26 §8) |

Integration 2026-10-30 (`integration/int126`): **0.36.126** is the combined source (counters v142, autshadow v15). Branch builds behind it: 0.36.121 `[EaCrashFixes]` (bugfix/ea-actor-pose-lifetime, v141), 0.36.122 mc12 and 0.36.123 mc13 (master, autshadow v13 / v14, contract 7 / 8), 0.36.124 `[RouteAsync] WorkerWait` (route branch, v142), 0.36.125 `[AutShadow] Satb` (autshadow v15). The played-households branch (played.cpp) kept its base version; it ships first in 0.36.126.

Integration 2026-10-30 (int129): **0.36.129** is the combined source (counters v143, autshadow v15). Branch builds behind it: 0.36.127 `[MonoFast] CallPath` (call fast path lane, v143, `asi.mcall1`), 0.36.128 `[Compaction]` (compaction lane, no native counters; ts3fixes 427-439).

0.36.133 (2026-10-30, docs/206 §9): Satb IC arguments logged per argument, docs/122 §14.23 consume counters; autshadow v16, ts3fixes contract 9 (no new ts3fixes counter slots, no main counter version).

0.36.136 (2026-10-30, docs/206 §10, docs/208 §8; 0.36.135 = `[MemDiag]` EA heap cores, docs/209): Satb sizes `MonoField.SetValueInternal` and `PtrToStructure(object)` (logged, no wait); autshadow v16 fields keep their slots, `satbRtFset` / `satbRtPtrStruct` now count sized calls (waits only in `satbRtWaits`). No new native counters; ts3fixes 481-490 (AutOffload apply).

0.36.144 (2026-10-31, docs/206 §16): Satb worker address-of on a changed static reads a cut-time copy (no give-up); no counter change.

0.36.146 (2026-10-31): docs/216 records every 256 cuts (native) / 250 results (ts3fixes); no counter change.

0.36.147 (2026-10-31, mono round 7): `[AutShadow] PreLearn` / `TierReqLots` (docs/216 rows 1 and 5, off by default, counts in the `autshadow r7` log line), `AutShadowNative.Config(31)` = 1 marks AddRow kind 3 (a request's scored LotId); no struct change, contract stays 10, no counter version.

0.36.145 (2026-10-31, docs/216 §5-§6): per-IC abort counts (stop excluded, `row` split) in `autshadow abortic` log lines every 2,048 request cuts (not in the counter block); first build with the go-live cost guard's `AutShadowNative.Config` keys 24-30 (docs/124 §17.10); no counter change.

0.36.143 (2026-10-31, docs/206 §15): Satb native writers (tick statics at the cut, binder body hook `0xE525C0`, static helpers, 4 more runtime icalls), `AsIcAbort` worker-only; no counter change; ts3fixes 534-537 (autofftick).

0.36.141 (2026-10-30, docs/30 §16, docs/206 §14): `[MonoThread] LazyEarly`, mtprobe v6 (sim lock waits, early gate-offs), Satb cold-transform names logged; no main counter version.

0.36.140 (2026-10-30, docs/206 §13): Satb worker arena chunk list deduplicated (256 distinct), no counter change.

0.36.139 (2026-10-30, docs/206 §12): Satb worker answers `MonoField.GetValue` from the cut (no give-up), give-ups logged by op; no counter change (0.36.138 = mono-cost17).

0.36.137 (2026-10-30, docs/122 §14.27, mono-cost16): `[AutShadow]` SimEvRows, TierMoveLot, ListAuditEvery, GoneDelta (all off by default); autshadow v18, no ts3fixes change, no main counter version.

0.36.138 (2026-10-30, docs/122 §14.28, mono-cost17): `[AutShadow]` IdsDelta, SimEvLearn, SimEvSeated, TierClassMax, GoneLoose (all off by default); autshadow v19, no ts3fixes change, no main counter version.

0.36.142 (2026-10-31, docs/124 §17): `[AutShadow] Mode=3` go-live consume (TopN, Bands, Margin, MaxWait, VerifyEvery, Dry; off by default), ObjStale watch map; autshadow v20, ts3fixes contract 10 (`LiveStat`, `LiveWatch`, `ObjStale`; counters in native `al[]`, no ts3fixes counter slots), no main counter version.

Next ts3native build after 0.36.143 (docs/124 §17.10, not built yet): `AutShadowNative.Config` keys 24-30 for the ts3fixes go-live cost guard (24 = 1 marker, cut us, cuts, the sim's GC lock wait us, the sim's locked GC entries, reference us, reference runs); no struct change, contract stays 10, no counter version.
0.36.149 (2026-10-30, docs/67 §28, docs/213 §8): `[EaCrashFixes] GeomBoneIndex` (the GEOM bone-by-index getter checked against the bone list; log lines only), `[EaCrashFixes] GeomNoMaterial` (issue #80, docs/67 §29: the Sim builder skips a part whose GEOM has no embedded material; log lines only) and `[EaCrashFixes] MintDivGuard` (issue #114: mint div.i4 / rem.i4 INT_MIN by -1; counters v144), all off by default.

Adding a version: append fields at the end of `Ts3nCounters` under a `// vN: ...` comment, set the first initializer value to N, update the "read N int32 (vN)" comment above the struct, bump `TS3NATIVE_VERSION`, add the `if (c.readS32() >= N) f.push(...)` line in `tools/probe/agent.js`, and add the row here. Runtime switches are `volatile LONG` and written by the probe (`/native?...`).

## ts3native `ts3native_mtprobe` export (`Ts3nMtProbe` in `native/ts3native/monothread-m5.cpp`)

Separate append-only struct of `LONG` for the second Mono thread (M5 build, docs/30 §14), so the routing lanes' `Ts3nCounters` versions stay untouched. `version` (offset 0) as above. Read by `tools/probe/agent.js` `nativeCounters` into `/native` as `mtprobe: {...}` (so a queue job's `-native.json` carries it); a new version appends its names to that list.

| Version | ts3native | Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|---|---|
| v1 | M5 branch (version set at merge) | 0x00-0x68 | `version`, `p3Ready` (bit 0 lock detours, 1 `0xE4B3F0` replaced, 2 `0xE4EC40` ret, 3 `GC_add_roots` left out), `p3Sites`, `locking` (gate, +0x0C), `lockedCalls`, `lockedWorker`, `lockWaits`, `lockWaitUs`, `lockMaxWaitUs`, `lockMaxDepth`, `lockOverflow` (tripwire), `finRuns`, `p4Ready` (+0x30; bits entry, exit A, exit B, `0xE534E0`), `p4Slots` (frame \| array << 8), `workerState` (+0x38), `workerTid`, `workerStarts`, `workerJobs`, `workerRunUs`, `workerMaxRunUs`, `workerExceptions`, `workerTrackSkips`, `workerStopFails`, `workerCollects`, `workerCtxSet` (tripwire), `seq` (+0x64), `result` |
| v2 | M5 G-A2 branch (version set at merge) | 0x6C-0x80 | `arenaRelNonEmpty`, `arenaDropped` (arena pool fix, `ArenaFix=1`), `n2mReady`, `n2mWorker`, `n2mMaxDepth`, `n2mOverflow` (tripwire) (F1 private call records, `N2mPrivate=1`; docs/120 §9) |
| v3 | 0.36.6 (P3 OOM branch, built as 0.36.4) | 0x84 | `lockStale` (locked P3 hook frames left by `longjmp`/unwind, dropped at the thread's next hooked enter/exit and their lock entry released; docs/120 §10, I-120-8) |
| v4 | 0.36.6 (P3 OOM branch, built as 0.36.5) | 0x88 | `lockOomReleased` (P3 shadow entries and lock recursions the out-of-memory thread released in the `GC_oom_fn` wrapper before calling `0xE78610`; taken back if it returns without `longjmp`; `p3Ready` bit 5 = slot wrapped; docs/120 §10.5) |
| v5 | 0.36.109 (docs/30 §15; read by agent.js) | 0x8C-0x97 | `lazyGate` (+0x8C; 1 = `[MonoThread] LazyGate=1`), `lazyOn` (dispatches that turned the gate on in `MtSubmit`), `lazyOff` (tick boundaries that turned it off with the worker idle) |
| v6 | 0.36.141 (docs/30 §16; read by agent.js) | 0x98-0xA7 | `simLockWaits` (+0x98; of `lockWaits`, threads other than the worker), `simLockWaitUs`, `simLockMaxWaitUs` (+0xA0), `lazyEarlyOff` (+0xA4; `LazyEarly=1`: gate off at a depth-0 sim entry once the worker is idle) |
| v7 | 0.36.256-quitstop1 (docs/30 §17; read by agent.js) | 0xA8-0xBB | `quitReady` (+0xA8; 1 = Mono shutdown entry `0xE534D0` detoured, `[MonoThread] QuitStop=1`), `quitStops` (shutdown entries that found no worker or stopped it), `quitTimeouts` (worker still inside after `QuitWaitMs`: left as it was), `quitWaitUs` (the stop's wait), `quitRestored` (+0xB8; bit 0 `0xE4B3F0` back to the game's code, bit 1 `[0x11949CC]` back to the game's root push) |
| v8 | 0.36.262-monogc1 (docs/30 §18; read by agent.js) | 0xBC-0xC7 | `workerAllocs` (+0xBC; allocation entries on the worker at hook depth 0), `workerAllocsTyped` (+0xC0; of those `GC_malloc_explicitly_typed`), `workerAllocMaxJob` (+0xC4; most in one job) |
| **v9** | next | 0xC8- | reserve here (the MtProbe job and Verify fields of docs/30 §7: `mtMismatches`, stress list) |

## ts3native `ts3native_autshadow` export (`Ts3nAutShadow` in `native/ts3native/autshadow.cpp`)

Separate append-only struct of `LONG` for the autonomy shadow pilot (docs/122 §13, `[AutShadow]`), like `ts3native_mtprobe`. `version` (offset 0) as above. Read by `tools/probe/agent.js` `nativeCounters` into `/native` as `autshadow: {...}` (v1 names, `auditMis` as an array, `abort` as `{reason: n}`, v2 names when `version` >= 2); a new version appends its names there. Abort reasons `abort[20]` in this order: `row`, `pos`, `memo`, `rng`, `slotpos`, `attach`, `query`, `ic`, `sf`, `privacy`, `roomcache`, `simstale`, `exc`, `stop`, `fmt`, `desirefn`, `punish`, `ref`, `list`, `other` (docs/122 §5.3's 16 plus F2/H5/H6's three and `ref`, `list`; `other` keeps anything else).

| Version | ts3native | Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|---|---|
| v1 | `[AutShadow]` part 1 branch (version set at merge) | 0x00-0xC8 | `version`, `ready` (bit 0 guards written, 1 F2 detour, 2 change tracking from routesnap's `World_FireEvent` detour, 3 object-event nodes), `guardSlots`, `guardChained` (slots that held routesnap's stub), `guardSkipped`, `descIcs`, `guardSim` (+0x18, copied at each cut), `guardWorker`, `workerHits`, `workerMisses`, `cuts` (+0x28), `cutUs`, `cutMaxUs`, `entries`, `refreshed`, `learned`, `learnDropped`, `epochs` (+0x44), `objEvents`, `dirtyOverflow`, `listGen`, `memoRecords`, `auditN` (+0x58), `auditMis[4]` (S1-S4), `auditSkipped` (+0x6C), `snapBytes`, `full`, `abortTotal` (+0x78), `abort[20]` (+0x7C) |
| v2 | `[AutShadow]` part 2 branch (version set at merge) | 0xCC-0x138 | `mode` (+0xCC, -1 = no cut: `[MonoThread]` slot stub missing), `req`, `busy`, `cutReq`, `refRuns` (+0xDC), `refUs`, `refMaxUs`, `refExc`, `rngDraws` (+0xEC, reference runs that moved the script RNG; restored), `workRuns`, `workUs`, `workMaxUs`, `workExc` (+0xFC), `workRefused`, `noWorker`, `windowUs` (+0x108), `windowMaxUs`, `overrun`, `epochDrops` (+0x114), `finishRuns`, `finishExc`, `stale` (+0x120, entries published stale at the last cut), `rooted` (bit 0 ref column + pools rooted, 1 array allocator, 2 empty string, 3 F2 exception), `icRegistered` (+0x128), `logLines`, `waitTicks`, `unguarded` (+0x134, reserved, not wired), `workerClassInit` (+0x138, reserved, not wired) |

## ts3native UserDiag ops (`UdTextMsg` in `native/ts3native/userdiag.cpp`)

ts3fixes `DiagSend(op, lo)` (`UserDiag.cs`): `Route.GetOption2` with optHi = `0x87000000 | op << 16`. One row per op; `node tools/registry.ts --self-check` fails when a `case N:` in `UdTextMsg` has no row here or two rows share an op.

| Op | Owner | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 0-2 | `UserDiag.cs` | text line: begin, append 4 bytes, end (logged as `ts3fixes: <line>`) |
| 3-5 | `memdiag.cpp` | [MemDiag] on / sample wanted / self-test wanted |
| 6 | `played.cpp` | store ready / household played (docs/14 §8.6) |
| 7 | `compaction.cpp` | [Compaction] verdict and steps (docs/204) |
| 8 | `autshadow-run.cpp` | [AutShadow] Unseen=1 asked (docs/222) |
| 10-11 | `UnseenFallback.cs` | UfWhy scope of route lo before its START or sync plan (docs/197 §7, docs/226) |
| 12 | `UnseenFallback.cs` | route lo's plan is over (docs/197 §7, docs/250 §7.0) |
| 13 | `UnseenFallback.cs` | seen NPC scope (docs/197 §7) |
| 14-19 | `castrim.cpp` | save sweep keep list and pump (docs/539 §6) |
| 20-29 | `discourage.cpp` | [Discourage] GhostTest |
| 30-34 | `discourage.cpp` | docs/89 §18 repro footprint window (`TS3N_RFTEST` builds only) |
| 35 | `gvro.cpp` | gvattr: the next plan's source class and scope (docs/250 §7.0) |
| 36 | `DoorGen.cs` / `hostcalls.cpp` | doorgen: hi 0 = lo 1 arms DoorMemoX=3, lo 0 bumps the managed door-input generation (older packages); hi 1 / 2 = the lot whose low dword is lo is held / free (an extra PortalPathPlan handler); hi 16 + source (docs/674 §9, `DoorShareCore.kSrc*`, `DS_SRC_OP_BASE`) = a bump from that source, lo = `DsIdHash` of the door or lot (0 = neither): DoorMemoX's generation moves on every one, `[RouteSnap] DoorShare`'s stamps by the source's scope |
| 37 | `Responsive631.cs` | playerfirst: route lo is player-ordered, for its next START; answers 1 when `[RouteAsync] PlayerFirst=1` (docs/631) |
| 40 | `doorshare.cpp` | doorshare (docs/674): the class of route lo's follower for its next START, hi 0-15 = bit 0 ordinary Sim, bit 1 the selected Sim's own command, bit 2 the follower is no Sim (boat, car), bits 3-15 a fold of the follower's object id; neither class bit set: bits 3-15 = why not (1 no description, 2 horse, 3 stray, 4 tomb mummy, 5 proprietor, 6 service, 7 IgnoreDoorLocks); answers 1 when `[RouteSnap] DoorShare` is on |
| 41 | `doorshare.cpp` | doorshare (docs/674): the door answer just given by `LotManager.PortalLockedCallback` is "unlocked" and does not depend on the router; lo = hash of the ask (portal id, router id, rooms), hi 0-15 = kind (1 plain door open to anybody, 2 portal no rule names, 3 any portal for a router that is no Sim) |
| 61 | `ModTime.cs` / `modtime.cpp` | [Diag] ModTime (docs/298): hi 0 on?, hi 1 sim hour `lo` starts (the previous one is written), hi 2 world quit, hi 3 notice text ready?, hi 4 bit `lo` of the notice text (61 keeps the number the 2026-11-20 diag kit used) |
| 62 | `PcCheck.cs` / `pccheck.cpp` | [PcCheck] (docs/299): hi 0 with lo 0 on?, with lo = one PC_* bit: problem set and Notify=1?; hi 1 sim hour `lo` ended; hi 2 world quit. 62 = the same op as on the docs/298 diag branch (61 = its [Diag] ModTime) |
| 63 | `AdaptDiag.cs` / `adaptdiag.cpp` | [AdaptDiag] (docs/316, kit diag ini only): hi&0xFF 0 activity `lo` (0xFF = probe: on?); 1 bit (hi>>8) of the install-check missing count; 2 world quit (report); 3 swapinvokediag asked this session?; 4 bit (hi>>8) of the kit-test InduceScriptErrors count |
| 48 | `FglInBound.cs` / `fglprof.cpp` | fglinbound (docs/682 §9): lo 1 = this thread's next FindGoodLocation search is `FindGoodLocationNearby`'s indoors stage with `IsInside` as its check (`[FglInside]` may answer facing tests on tiles without a room), lo 0 = over; answers 1 when `[FglInside]` is installed |
| **38-39, 42-47, 49-60, 63+** | next free | add a row with the `case` (38-39 and 42-47 are in use on contributor branches not yet in this repo) |

## ts3native `ts3native_readydrain` export (`ReadyDrainCounters` in `native/ts3native/ts3native.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, not versioned (branch feat/route-ready-drain-1115, `[RouteAsync] ReadyDrain`). Append fields at the end only.

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x2B | `mode` (ReadyDrain), `budgetUs` (ReadyDrainUs), `polls`, `served`, `saved` (polls saved), `budgetHits`, `maxUs`, `frameUs` (ReadyDrainFrameUs), `gapUs` (ReadyDrainGapUs), `frameHits`, `idleStops` |

## ts3native `ts3native_taskserve` export (`TaskServeCounters` in `native/ts3native/ts3native.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, not versioned (`[RouteAsync] TaskServe`, docs/637, taskserve.cpp). Append fields at the end only. No ts3fixes counter slot (native only; the managed side is unchanged).

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x1B | `mode` (TaskServe, runtime switch), `ready` (call sites patched), `checks` (task runs with a call posted), `served`, `skipped` (posted but not servable there), `queueUs`, `maxQueueUs` (post -> pick-up of the calls served there) |

## ts3native `ts3native_closestwps` export (`ClosestWpsCounters` in `native/ts3native/ts3native.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, not versioned (`[RouteAsync] ClosestWpsCut`, plandeadline.cpp `CowFindSkip`, contributor 2026-11-20). Append fields at the end only. No ts3fixes counter slot (native only). Logged in the minute counter line "closest waypoint cut:".

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x0B | `mode` (1 = cut, 0 = count only; set at install from the ini), `cuts` (ClosestWaypoints_Find calls answered "no waypoint" past the deadline of a job whose route is known unseen, no wait's ask), `would` (the same calls with mode 0) |

## ts3native `ts3native_doorgen` export (`DmxScriptGen` in `native/ts3native/ts3native.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, not versioned (`[RouteSnap] DoorMemoX=3`, switch `doorgen`). No ts3fixes counter slot: native counts the bumps.

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x0F | `gen` (managed door-input generation), `armed` (op 36 lo 1 seen this session), `bumps`, `unarmedAsks` (mode 3 asks before arming, run as mode 0) |
| 0x10-0x1F | `missCold` (no row for the key), `missGen` (row of another generation / delegate set), `missAge` (row older than DoorMemoXMs), `notUnlocked` (live answers not "unlocked", never stored); any DoorMemoX mode, "door memo x misses:" line |
| 0x20-0x23 | `exempt`: mode 1 / 3 asks of a household, selected or unknown-scope job, asked live (docs/585 §5); "door memo x mode 3:" line |
| 0x24-0x27 | `held`: mode 3 asks on a lot with a privacy / mod door handler (op 36 hi 1), asked live, not kept or compared (docs/585 §6); "door memo x misses:" line |

## ts3native `ts3native_slavelink` export (`SlaveLinkCounters` in `native/ts3native/eafix.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, not versioned (`[EaCrashFixes] SlaveUnlink` and `DeadSlaveTrip`, docs/143 §8.11). Append fields at the end only. The `ts3native_counters` layout is unchanged.

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x17 | `unlinkOn` (SlaveUnlink installed), `tripOn` (DeadSlaveTrip installed), `sets` (slave lists recorded at Route.Start, 0x955809), `unlinks` (destroyed slaves taken off a live master list; "ea fix: SlaveUnlink" lines), `overflow` (slaves not recorded: 64-pair table full), `tripHits` (destroyed slave answered null at an avoidance read; "ea fix: DeadSlaveTrip" lines, stays 0 with SlaveUnlink=1) |
| 0x18-0x23 | `cap` (pairs the table holds now: 64, doubled when every pair is live, up to 65,536), `hw` (most pairs held at once), `grows` (times it doubled; "ea fix: SlaveUnlink: ... table grown" lines); docs/321 H4, 0.36.299-ucap1. `overflow` now counts only lists refused at the 65,536-pair ceiling |

## ts3native `ts3native_caps` export (`Ts3nCaps` in `native/ts3native/caps.h`)

docs/321 lane 0 (0.36.299-ucap1; rows 17-18 since ucap2): fill of our own fixed-size tables, always on, measurement only. `version` (1), `n` (rows), then per row three `LONG`: `cap` (rows now), `hw` (most rows at once), `full` (an add refused, a live row evicted or a fallback taken). Rows in `CAP_*` order; append rows at the end only. The same values go to ts3native.log as `caps (minute, docs/321 lane 0: name hw/cap full): slave .. kd .. ...`, at most once a minute and only after a change.

| Row | Name | Table (docs/321 item) | `full` means |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `slave` | eafix.cpp SlaveUnlink pairs (H4, grows) | a list refused at the ceiling |
| 1 | `kd` | plandeadline.cpp KindDeadlines rows cut in the last 30 s (H2, grows) | a live row evicted at the 4096-row ceiling |
| 2 | `asSet` | autshadow.cpp fullest AsSet of 3072 ids | an add refused (any set) |
| 3 | `asSimIds` | autshadow.cpp simIds (M1, never cleared) | - |
| 4 | `asOl` | autshadow.cpp object-lot map, 3/4 of its table (M2; ucap2: the table grows from 65,536 to 262,144 rows, cap = 196,608) | a row refused |
| 5 | `asTier` | autshadow.cpp g_asTierTab 1024 (M3) | a Sim left at tier 1 |
| 6 | `asLotTier` | autshadow.cpp g_asLotTier 512 (M4) | a lot left at tier 1 |
| 7 | `utb` | unseentable.cpp rows per generation 1024 (M6) | the old rule answered |
| 8 | `utbWalk` | unseentable.cpp agents walked 4096 (M6) | the walk stopped |
| 9 | `jobs` | ts3native.cpp route jobs queued or running, 8 or 32 (M7) | asyncQueueFull |
| 10 | `rd` | replandedupe.cpp 256 rows, 4-way (M8; hw not kept) | a row inside its 1 s window evicted |
| 11 | `spDoor` | snap.cpp door lots per session 512 (M9) | a lot not recorded |
| 12 | `spMB` | snap.cpp P3 image MB vs CapMB (M10) | P3 switched off |
| 13 | `dmx` | hostcalls.cpp DoorMemoX ring 256 (M11; hw not kept; ucap2: grows to 4096, see row 17) | a row inside its life overwritten (ucap2: only at the 4096 ceiling) |
| 14 | `dmxHold` | hostcalls.cpp held lots 32 (M11) | every lot held |
| 15 | `aa` | unseenanim.cpp 4096 slots (L4; hw not kept; ucap2: hashed, 16-slot probe chains) | a collision hold (ucap2: all 16 slots of the chain owed) |
| 16 | `gvu` | unseenfb.cpp deferred unseen routes 32 (L5) | a live streak evicted |
| 17 | `dmxLive` | hostcalls.cpp DoorMemoX live rows at a put, of the 4096 ceiling (M11 lane C, ucap2) | - (see row 13) |
| 18 | `aaDisp` | unseenanim.cpp distinct actors whose home slot another actor owed in (L4 lane C, ucap2; cleared with the table) | - (see row 15) |

## ts3native `ts3native_routespeed` export (`RouteSpeedCounters` in `native/ts3native/routespeed.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, not versioned (`[RouteSpeed] NeighbourMemo`, docs/663). Append fields at the end only. No ts3fixes counter slot (native only); the harness flips `nmMode` by export name (`native_set neighbourmemo`).

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x1F | `nmMode` (0 off, 1 serve, 2 shadow; runtime switch), `nmReady` (bit 0 neighbour hook, bit 1 table-clear hook, bit 2 tree-write sequence), `nmCalls`, `nmHits`, `nmServed` (cells written by served answers), `nmStores`, `nmResets`, `nmCapMiss` (out vector had no room) |
| 0x20-0x3F | `nmScopeMiss` (household / selected / unknown router), `nmNoStore`, `nmChecks` (shadow compares), `nmMismatch` (must stay 0), `nmNoMemo` (thread past 4), `nmMissUs` / `nmMissN` (sampled uncached call time), `nmLines` |

## ts3native `ts3native_hitchsum` export (`HsTotals` in `native/ts3native/hitchsum.cpp`)

Separate struct of `LONG`, `version` 1 (`[HitchAttr] HitchMs` / `RouteLockMs`, docs/666 §5; log only). No ts3fixes counter slot. Layout asserted in hitchsum.cpp.

| Offsets | Fields |
|---|---|
| 0x00-0x17 | `version`, `hitchUs` (HitchMs), `lockLogUs` (RouteLockMs), `frames`, `hitches`, `hitchMs` |
| 0x18-0x67 | `causeN[10]`, `causeMs[10]` (by cause class: sync-plan / job-wait / lock918 / other-lock / gc / limiter / sim-cpu / off-cpu / mixed / unknown) |
| 0x68-0xAF | `+0x918` waits of the sim / main thread: `lkWaits`, `lkUs`, `lkMaxUs`, `lkLines`, `lkDropped`, `lkWorkerN/Us`, `lkJobEndN/Us`, `lkJob250N/Us`, `lkOtherN/Us`, `lkMainN/Us`; `hitchLock250`; `lines`, `dropped` ("hitch:" lines) |
